# Gateway write-route inventory

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| VTID | VTID-05698 |
| Plan | `gateway-write-route-auth`, lane `write-route-inventory` (`.kiro/specs/gateway-write-route-auth/plan.md` on branch `kiro/gateway-write-route-auth/plan`, §2 and §4) |
| Repository | `exafyltd/vitana-platform` |
| Pin | `6dc6e1ba0c18252ec2f3fbf5651d245e200f9835` (`origin/main`). Every `path:line` below is read at this SHA. |
| Other repo read | `vitana-v1` at `e31bf4627ff3c705c20f8e6b718ce4dd03353b7f` (`origin/main`), directories `src`, `apps`, `supabase` |
| Method | Code reading only. No request was sent to any live system. No code was changed. |
| Sibling lane | VTID-05697 (lane `route-auth`, PR #4955) gates six routes; they are listed here as "in progress (VTID-05697)". |

## Status classes

Every claim in this report carries the strongest status the evidence supports.

| Status class | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Verified operational | Seen working on a live system, with a run id. **No claim in this report has this status**: nothing was probed. |
| Verified implemented, not operational | Read in the code at the pin; not observed live. This is the status of every route verdict below. |
| Implemented but incomplete | Code exists but a switch, secret or caller change is missing, e.g. a gate that runs in `log` mode. |
| Designed but not implemented | Described in a plan or comment; no code at the pin. |
| Deprecated/superseded | Code that still answers but is retired or replaced (e.g. a 410 route). |
| Unknown | Cannot be decided from the code (live config, upstream network rules). |

Live exposure of every route is **Unknown**: Cloud Run services are deployed with `--allow-unauthenticated` (`scripts/deploy/deploy-service.sh:152,168,183,193`), and the repo holds no gateway ALB, WAF, CloudFront or API Gateway rule. Whether anything upstream blocks these paths in AWS was not checked (plan §2).

## Verdicts

| Verdict | Meaning |
|---|---|
| gated | An existing middleware rejects an unauthenticated request before the handler runs: inline on the route, a `router.use` on the same router registered before the route, or a `router.use` of a router mounted earlier on the same prefix. |
| gated in handler | No middleware gate, but the handler refuses an unauthenticated request before any write: it verifies the JWT (`verifyAndExtractIdentity`, `auth.getUser()`, `verifyAuth`, `getUserContext`), forwards the bearer to Supabase as the user JWT (PostgREST verifies it), or checks a shared secret. |
| public by design | Deliberately anonymous, with the reason stated: signed webhook, auth bootstrap, analytics or diagnostic beacon, anonymous landing-page ORB session, 405 or 410 responder. |
| unauthenticated and needs a gate | An anonymous caller (or one with a self-made token) reaches the write. Severity, the existing gate to reuse and the callers are given. |
| in progress (VTID-05697) | One of the six routes gated by the sibling lane. |
| Unknown | Could not be decided from the code. **No route needed this verdict**; the open questions are configuration questions, listed in §9. |

Severity of "needs a gate", by effect:

| Severity | Effect |
|---|---|
| critical | Reaches money or payment state, the autonomous code pipeline (claims, dispatch, self-healing), or reads/writes any member's data by naming their id. |
| high | Changes state for all members or another member (mass messages, ledger and pipeline state, admin config, another member's data), or drives LLM/browser cost or a deploy trigger. |
| medium | Bounded integrity or cost effect: audit or registry records, a single job run, impersonated low-value actions. |
| low | Telemetry, in-memory state, pure computation, housekeeping. |

Path conventions in this report: a citation is relative to the repository root; when no such file exists it is relative to `services/gateway/src/` (so `routes/x.ts`, `middleware/x.ts` and `index.ts` are gateway files). `hub:N` means `services/gateway/src/frontend/command-hub/app.js` line N. `vitana-v1/...` is a path in the vitana-v1 checkout at the SHA above. `docs/validation/VTID-05698/check-citations.mjs` checks every citation against these rules.

## 1. Summary

Counts are per table row (one row per route definition; a definition mounted on several paths, such as `cicdRouter`, is one row). 837 rows cover 855 method-and-path endpoints.

| Verdict | Rows |
|---|---|
| gated | 469 |
| gated in handler | 214 |
| public by design | 44 |
| unauthenticated and needs a gate | 104 (critical 7, high 47, medium 21, low 29) |
| in progress (VTID-05697) | 6 |
| Unknown | 0 |
| **Total** | **837** |

Not counted: `POST /meetups/:id/rsvp` on `communityMeetupRouter` (`routes/live.ts:2593`), which is never mounted (§2).

The seven critical routes:

- `POST /api/v1/events/ingest` (`routes/events.ts:42`): anonymous; a `source=routine.*` event with status `warning` or `error` runs `processFailingService()` (`routes/events.ts:133-163`), which allocates a VTID and starts LLM diagnosis and auto-fix-or-escalate with caller-supplied text in the prompt.
- `POST /api/v1/memory/semantic/search`, `/write`, `/context` (`routes/semantic-memory.ts:169,251,334`): anonymous; the caller names any `tenant_id`/`user_id` in the body lens (`routes/semantic-memory.ts:55`) and the service-role client reads or writes that member's `memory_items` (`services/gateway/src/services/supabase-semantic-memory.ts:275-287`).
- `POST /webhooks/stripe-dance` (`routes/payments-stripe-webhook.ts:42`): checks only that a `stripe-signature` header exists (`:43-47`), never verifies it, and moves `service_payments` state from the posted event (`:51-112`).
- `POST /api/v1/worker/orchestrator/route` and `/tasks/:vtid/claim` (`routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:223,1230`): anonymous; only the execution-armed control stands in front of work-order dispatch and VTID claims (§7).

A class of routes accepts a token it never verifies: the handler base64-decodes the JWT (`jose.decodeJwt` or `split('.')[1]`) and then writes with the service-role client. A self-made token passes, and `delegatedTokenGuard` lets it through because its `iss` is not this project (`services/gateway/src/services/delegation-guard.ts:107`). These routes are marked "needs a gate": `routes/admin/ai-integrations.ts:68,119` (admin flag read from unsigned claims, `:29-48`), `routes/consent-actions.ts:45,52,69` (`:22-36`), `routes/user-limitations.ts:89` (`:20-34`), `routes/wearables.ts:123,278`, `routes/wearables-waitlist.ts:55,89`, `routes/community.ts:874,949,1018`. Routes that decode the token only after Supabase has accepted it (for notification fan-out) are "gated in handler", e.g. `routes/live.ts:1672` before `:1721`.

Two existing gates run in `log` mode by default, which lets anonymous calls through: `requireLedgerWriteAuth` (`middleware/ledger-write-auth.ts:39-44,93-99`; production `log` per `.claude/rules/infrastructure.md:263`, staging `enforce` per `.github/workflows/AWS-STAGE-DEPLOY-GATEWAY.yml:1286`) and `requireScheduledNotificationsAuth` (`middleware/scheduled-notifications-auth.ts:44-51,104-110`; `log` in both environments per `.claude/rules/infrastructure.md:268` and `.github/workflows/AWS-PROD-DEPLOY-GATEWAY.yml:946-947`). Their 23 routes are "needs a gate"; the fix is an owner-approved switch to `enforce`, not new code. Three scheduled-notification ticks check the token in the handler whatever the mode (`routes/scheduled-notifications.ts:1728,1755,1784`) and are "gated in handler".

## 2. Method

1. **Route extraction.** A TypeScript-AST pass (the `typescript` compiler API, no type checking) over every `services/gateway/src/routes/**/*.ts` file except `*.test.ts` and `__tests__`, collecting each `<router>.post|put|patch|delete|all(path, ...middleware, handler)` call, each `router.use(...)`, the router variables (`Router()` / `express.Router()`) and the exports. The same pass reads `index.ts` for `require`/`import` bindings, every `mountRouterSync(app, path, router)` and `app.use(path, ...)`, and inline `app.post|put|patch|delete` routes. Result: 829 route calls in route files, 1 inline route (`index.ts:1157`), 304 `mountRouterSync` mounts. A line grep for `.(post|put|patch|delete)(` finds the same route calls plus Supabase `.delete()` and `Map.delete()` calls (`commands.log`).
2. **Mount paths.** Full path = mount path + route path. Sub-routers mounted inside route files were followed by hand: `routes/chat.ts:95,97` (`/search`, `/viewing`), `routes/partner-onboarding-connections.ts:222-238` and `routes/vcaop-portal-my.ts:246,504` (`registerConnectionRoutes` registers the same nine connection routes on two routers with different guards; both are listed). A router mounted on several paths (`cicdRouter` at `index.ts:820-822`) is one table row with all paths. `communityMeetupRouter` (`routes/live.ts:2586-2593`) is imported at `index.ts:289` but never mounted, so `POST .../meetups/:id/rsvp` is not reachable at the pin; it is not counted.
3. **Global middleware.** `index.ts:598-640`: CORS, SSE headers, compression, raw/text body parsers for webhook paths (`:614-632`), `express.json` (`:635`), `delegatedTokenGuard()` (`:640`). None authenticates; the guard calls `next()` without a bearer (`services/gateway/src/services/delegation-guard.ts:151-158`) and skips tokens from other issuers (`:107`). `mountRouterSync` only records routes for duplicate detection and calls `app.use(path, router)` (`services/gateway/src/governance/route-guard.ts:248-312`); no mount adds middleware. The only path-scoped `app.use` that is not a body parser is the GitLab delivery stage (`index.ts:1292`), which sits behind `requireGitlabSignature` on its route.
4. **Gate detection.** A route is "gated" when an inline middleware or an earlier `router.use` on its router is one of the gates read in `services/gateway/src/middleware/` (`requireAuth`, `requireAuthWithTenant`, `requireAdminAuth`, `requireExafyAdmin`, `requireTenant`, `requireServiceOrAdmin`, `requireAdminOnly`, `requireTenantAdmin`, `requirePlatformScope`, `requireOwnSession`, `requireEntitlement`) or a local gate whose body was read: `requireDevRole` (`routes/dev-autopilot.ts:121-139`, `routes/autonomy-pulse.ts:47-65`, `routes/orchestrator.ts:56-72`), `requireOrgAdmin` (`routes/partner-orgs.ts:90-107`), `requireInternalOrAdmin` (`routes/automations.ts:59-65`), `requireIngestAuth` (`routes/catalog-ingest.ts:52-93`), `requirePartnerHealthAccess` (`routes/admin-partner-health.ts:81-100`), `requireRoutineToken` (`routes/routines.ts:110-125`), `livekitTestsAuthGate` (`routes/voice-lab.ts:114-196`), `serviceTokenAuth` (`routes/admin-staging.ts:41-54`), `requireScanToken` (`routes/dev-autopilot.ts:154-164`), `requireApiKey` (`routes/screen-load-health.ts:90-103`), `requireServiceToken` (`routes/autopilot.ts:124-177`, `routes/execute.ts:60-77`, `routes/auth-bridge.ts:66-77`), `requireDeveloperAccess` (`routes/command-hub.ts:27-62`), `requireDevMemoryWriter` (`routes/dev-memory.ts:52-70`), `requireGitlabSignature` (`routes/internal-gitlab-webhook.ts:59-78`), `emitAuthGate` (`routes/oasis-emit.ts:146-187`), `requireSessionToken` (`routes/watcher.ts:294-311`), `requireVoiceLabDevAccess` (`routes/voice-lab.ts:513-536`), `requireSupervisorAccess` (`routes/voice-supervisor.ts:83-119`), `bindConversationBodyIdentity` (`routes/conversation-identity.ts:42-87`), and the router-level `optionalAuth` + "401 unless `req.identity.user_id`" pairs (`routes/calendar.ts:154,169-175`, `routes/reminders.ts:66-77`, `routes/recommendation-inbox.ts:45-52`, `routes/autopilot-recommendations.ts:204-218`). `optionalAuth`, `operatorMachineAuth` (`routes/operator.ts:129-138`), `probeMiddleware` (`routes/commerce-mcp.ts:213-225`), rate limiters and `stagingOnlyGuard` are not gates.
5. **Cross-router blanket gates.** A path-less `router.use(gate)` runs for every request that enters the router, also for paths that a later router on the same prefix serves. Three cases at the pin: `routes/feedback-admin.ts:34` (`requireAdminAuth`, mounted `index.ts:1556`) gates `routes/feedback-actions.ts` admin routes (mounted `index.ts:1560`); `routes/specialists-admin.ts:46` (`requireAdminAuth`, `index.ts:1564`) gates `routes/specialists-connections.ts` (`index.ts:1566`); `routes/autopilot.ts:178` (`requireServiceToken`, `index.ts:915`) would gate the prompts and recommendations routers, but it hands `/prefs`, `/prompts/*` and `/recommendations/*` on with `next('router')` (`routes/autopilot.ts:147-163`), so those routes rely on their own checks.
6. **Handler review.** Every route without a middleware gate of its own (353 table rows) was read, and so were the 23 rows behind a `log`-mode gate. Signals extracted per handler and its same-file helpers (`commands.log` lists the categoriser): JWT verification, bearer forwarded to a user-JWT Supabase client or RPC, unverified decode, `401 when req.identity is absent`, shared secret or signature, fallback actors such as `'developer'`. Each route with no signal, or with an unverified decode, a presence-only token check, a fallback actor or a secret, was read by hand and has a manual verdict (146 rows); the handler-level rows in the table cite the line of the check.
7. **Effect column.** For manually decided rows the effect is written by hand with citations. For the other rows it is extracted from the handler and same-file helpers and repository functions: tables (`.from('x')`, `rest/v1/x`), RPCs, awaited service calls and OASIS emission; these cells end in "(auto)".
8. **Callers column.** Literal path references: the full path (static prefix up to the first parameter, plus the static tail segments) grepped over the repository's code files (excluding `routes/`, tests, docs, SQL, JSON) and over vitana-v1 `src apps supabase` lines that contain `/api/v1/`. The method is not filtered, so a path's GET callers show up too. For every "needs a gate" row a second grep used the path without `/api/v1` (vitana-v1 mobile calls `getJson('/user/limitations')`, `vitana-v1/apps/mobile/src/screens/settings/data/hooks.ts:525`). Callers that build the path from a variable base are missed by both greps; `services/openclaw-bridge/src/skills/vitana-llm-routing.ts:57-63` is the known case (§6).
9. **Heuristic cross-check.** The plan's heuristic count (123 routes in 45 files) came from a command that is not on the plan branch. `commands.log` records the variants tried and one reproducible command, with its hit list resolved against this table. All 837 rows have a verdict, so every hit of any of those variants is resolved.

## 3. Cross-cutting findings

- **Unverified JWT decode with service-role writes** (§1). Thirteen routes; status: Verified implemented, not operational. The fix in each case is the existing `requireAuth` (or `requireAdminAuth` for `routes/admin/ai-integrations.ts`) and using `req.identity.user_id`. The callers already send the member's real JWT (`hub:29170`, `hub:29233`, `vitana-v1/apps/mobile/src/screens/settings/data/requests.ts:83`), so no caller change is expected.
- **Gates in `log` mode** (§1). Status: Implemented but incomplete. VTID-04727 changed every in-repo ledger caller to send a credential (`docs/CHANGELOG.md:28`); whether production logs still show unknown callers is Unknown.
- **dev-sandbox bypass.** Many D-series routes skip the bearer check when `ENVIRONMENT` or `VITANA_ENV` contains `dev` or `sandbox` and then write with the service-role client under a fixed dev identity (e.g. `routes/availability-readiness.ts:61-69`, `services/gateway/src/services/d28-emotional-cognitive-engine.ts:125-167`, `services/gateway/src/services/d49-risk-mitigation-engine.ts:142-151`; ORB memory identity `routes/orb-live.ts:614-639`). `scripts/deploy/deploy-service.sh:86,154` defaults Cloud Run deploys to `ENVIRONMENT=dev-sandbox`. The AWS production task's `ENVIRONMENT`/`VITANA_ENV` value is not in the repo: **Unknown**. These routes are "gated in handler" on the assumption that production is not dev-sandbox; a follow-up confirms it (FU-15).
- **Fail-open service check.** `routes/architecture-investigator.ts:33-41` accepts any non-empty `x-service-auth` header when `SERVICE_AUTH_TOKEN` is unset (`:40`); that variable is set in no deploy workflow or infrastructure rule found.
- **Header-supplied roles left outside the six routes.** `routes/operator.ts:141-151` takes the operator id and role from `x-operator-id` / `x-operator-role` for `/deploy` and `/command`. VTID-05697 removes the `x-actor-role` reads in `routes/llm.ts` only.
- **Cognito admins.** Cognito tokens never carry `exafy_admin` (`middleware/auth-supabase-jwt.ts:253-267`), so every exafy_admin gate proposed below locks Cognito-signed-in admins out until that known gap is fixed. Unchanged by this lane.
- **Tenant-admin check without the permitted-roles step.** `routes/tenant-specialists.ts:53-75` verifies the JWT but accepts `active_role === 'admin'` alone (`:69`), unlike `middleware/require-tenant-admin.ts:143-157`. Gated in handler; noted for the owner.

## 5. GET routes that serve data produced by the six VTID-05697 routes

VTID-05697 gates the writes. These reads stay open at the pin; none of them is a write route, so they are not in the counts.

| Route | Definition | Serves | Gate at the pin | Severity | Proposed gate |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/v1/agents/triage/:sessionId/stream` | `routes/triage-agent.ts:351` | SSE output of the investigation started by `POST /investigate`: the LLM analysis of a voice session and the OASIS events it pulled. For Anthropic sessions the stream also answers the agent's `query_oasis_events` tool calls with service credentials (`routes/triage-agent.ts:473-491`), so opening it keeps the paid session running. | none | **medium**: the session id is a random UUID (`router-<uuid>`, `routes/triage-agent.ts:216`) or an Anthropic session id, returned only to the `/investigate` caller (`:238,339`); once VTID-05697 gates `/investigate`, a reader needs a leaked id | short-lived stream token (below) |
| `GET /api/v1/specs/:vtid` | `routes/specs.ts:1342` | spec markdown written by `PATCH /api/v1/specs/:vtid` | none; service-role read (`:1356-1360`) | **medium**: VTIDs are sequential (`VTID-XXXXX`), so every spec is enumerable | `requireServiceOrAdmin`, after checking the Command Hub GET callers (`hub:9432,9775,9938`) send `buildContextHeaders` |
| `GET /api/v1/llm/routing-policy/audit` | `routes/llm.ts:296` | audit history of routing-policy changes, including actor ids | none | **low** | `requireServiceOrAdmin` |
| `GET /api/v1/llm/routing-policy` | `routes/llm.ts:134` | the active routing policy | none | **low**: configuration, no member data; openclaw-bridge `get_policy` reads it without a token (`services/openclaw-bridge/src/skills/vitana-llm-routing.ts:79-86`) | leave open or `requireServiceOrAdmin` once openclaw-bridge has a credential (§6) |
| `GET /api/v1/ai-personality`, `GET /api/v1/ai-personality/:surface_key` | `routes/ai-personality.ts:28,45` | assistant personality configs written by PUT and reset | none | **low**: prompt configuration, no member data | owner decision; `requireServiceOrAdmin` if the configs are not meant to be public |

**Triage stream gate design.** `EventSource` cannot send an `Authorization` header, so the stream cannot reuse a bearer middleware as-is. Options:

1. **Single-session stream token (recommended).** The gated `POST /investigate` mints an HMAC token bound to `{session_id, admin user_id, expiry}` and returns `stream_url: /api/v1/agents/triage/<id>/stream?st=<token>`. The stream handler verifies the HMAC, the session id match and the expiry before writing any SSE header (`routes/triage-agent.ts:355-359` sets headers first today). TTL in minutes (the router cache already expires after 10 minutes, `routes/triage-agent.ts:149`). The repo has the pattern: `services/gateway/src/services/kiro/kiro-mcp-token.ts:47-70` mints and verifies an HMAC pass with `createHmac` and `timingSafeEqual`. No change to the Command Hub caller: it already opens `new EventSource(result.stream_url)` (`hub:18550`). A leaked token is useless for another session or after it expires.
2. **Supabase JWT in the query string.** The ORB live stream does this (`routes/orb-live.ts:16408,16419` + `verifyAndExtractIdentity`). It needs a Command Hub change to append the token, and it puts a long-lived bearer in URLs and access logs. Not recommended for an admin token.
3. **Cookie.** No gateway route uses a session cookie today; this would be a new identity mechanism, which the plan rules out (§1 of the plan).

The same residual exists for `GET /api/v1/reminders/stream`, which falls back to a `?user_id=` query parameter (`routes/reminders.ts:38-53,183-185`); its own comment names a signed short-TTL token as the fix. It is not produced by the six routes and is listed only as a related follow-up (FU-13).

## 6. openclaw-bridge routing-policy writes

`services/openclaw-bridge/src/skills/vitana-llm-routing.ts` calls the gateway through `callGateway()` (`:57-69`), which sends only `Content-Type` (`:61`) and builds the URL from `GATEWAY_URL` + `/api/v1/llm` + path (`:59`). `update_policy` posts `/routing-policy` (`:94`) and `reset_policy` posts `/routing-policy/reset` (`:121`), each followed by an `autopilot_logs` insert with the service role (`:104-109`, `:127-132`). A literal grep for `/api/v1/llm/routing-policy` does not find these calls because the path is split.

After VTID-05697 both gateway routes require `requireServiceOrAdmin`, so these two actions get 401 and fail closed through the existing error path (`:64-67`); `get_policy` (`:79-86`) keeps working. Proposed follow-up (FU-14): give openclaw-bridge the gateway service credential and send `Authorization: Bearer <GATEWAY_SERVICE_TOKEN>` from `callGateway()` (the gateway's own header helper is `middleware/require-service-or-admin.ts:63-66`), or retire the two write actions. **Which one is the owner's decision**: it is a credential change (plan §1, §4 lane `route-auth`). Severity of leaving it as is: low (the autopilot skill cannot change routing policy; nothing is exposed).

## 7. Worker-orchestrator routes and the autonomy risk register

The autonomy risk register of VTID-05686 (`docs/programs/strategy-execution/autonomy-risk-register.md` on branch `kiro/strategy-execution-wave1/autonomy-risks`, not on `main` at the pin) lists **AR-07, "Worker claim fails open"** (critical) at its line 58: if the ledger read at `routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:1260-1264` fails or returns no row, `ledgerRow` is undefined and the autonomy-ownership check is skipped before `claim_vtid_task` runs (`:1290`). AR-07 frames the caller as "any `service_role` caller". At the route level the exposure is wider: **no worker-orchestrator write route has any authentication**, so the caller can be anyone who reaches the gateway. The only control in front of a claim or a dispatch is the `autopilot_execution_enabled` control (`routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:1233-1242`, `:226-229`), whose live value the register records as Unknown.

| # | Route | Definition | Relation to the register |
|---|---|---|---|
| 832 | `POST /api/v1/worker/orchestrator/tasks/:vtid/claim` | `routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:1230` | AR-07: the fail-open check plus no caller authentication. Fixing AR-07 alone (fail closed, predicate in the RPC) still leaves an anonymous caller able to claim autonomous-plane work. |
| 830, 831 | `POST /register`, `DELETE /register/:worker_id` | `routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:891,989` | `await-autopilot-execution` (`:1600-1616`) and progress (`:1395`) trust a registered `worker_id`; registration itself is anonymous, so worker ids are not credentials. |
| 826 | `POST /api/v1/worker/orchestrator/route` | `routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:223` | dispatch of work orders while execution is armed |
| 828, 829 | subagent and orchestrator `complete` | `routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:362,519` | forged completion and verification of autonomous work |
| 833, 834, 835 | release, progress, heartbeat | `routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:1332,1379,1447` | claim lifecycle |
| 827, 836, 837 | subagent start, await-autopilot-execution, cleanup | `routes/worker-orchestrator.ts:317,1589,1715` | events, long-poll read, housekeeping |

AR-12 in the same register records that `services/worker-runner/` has no source at HEAD, so the real worker caller of claim, register and heartbeat cannot be read. In-repo callers that would need the service token: `mcp/vitana-work/src/lib/gateway-client.ts:144,150` (sends `Authorization` only when an API key is configured, `:83-90`), `services/gateway/src/services/orb-tools/worker-orchestrator-tools.ts:11,70,86,122,135`, `services/gateway/src/services/orb-tools/vtid-lifecycle-tools.ts:347,414,428`, `services/gateway/src/services/autopilot-event-loop.ts:530`, `services/gateway/src/services/test-contract-commands.ts:230`. Proposed: one sparred plan that adds `requireServiceOrAdmin` to all twelve routes and implements the AR-07 fix together (FU-2).

## 8. Proposed follow-ups

Each follow-up needs its own sparred plan and VTID. None is done by this lane. "Approval gate" is what has to happen before the change ships.

| ID | Title | Routes (table #) | Files | Severity | Proposed existing gate | Callers to change | Owner | Approval gate |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FU-1 | Authenticate OASIS event ingest and VTID lifecycle writes | 273-279 | `routes/events.ts` | critical | `requireServiceOrAdmin` | `.github/actions/emit-deploy-telemetry/action.yml:89-91` (curl without Authorization), `packages/agent-heartbeat.ts:96`, `services/agents/shared/llm_telemetry.py:389`, `services/agents/vitana-orchestrator/src/vitana_orchestrator/stage_gate.py:382`, `mcp/vitana-work/src/lib/gateway-client.ts:212`, `hub:10196,24936` and the `hub` `/api/v1/oasis/events` writers; the daily routines that post `source=routine.*` (not in this repo) | OASIS owner; autonomy owner | sparred plan; service-token distribution to CI and agents is a credential change (owner) |

## 9. Unknown

- Live exposure of every route, and any upstream AWS rule in front of the gateway (§ Status classes).
- Live values of `LEDGER_WRITE_AUTH_MODE` and `SCHEDULED_NOTIFICATIONS_AUTH_MODE` in production (the repo records `log`).
- Production `ENVIRONMENT` / `VITANA_ENV` (decides whether the dev-sandbox bypasses are active).
- Whether `SERVICE_AUTH_TOKEN` is set in production (decides whether `routes/architecture-investigator.ts:40` fails open).
- Live value of `autopilot_execution_enabled` (the only control in front of worker claims and dispatch).
- Who calls the semantic-memory, consent-action and signup-result routes in production: no code caller was found in either repository.
- The worker-runner caller of the worker routes (no source, AR-12).
- Whether `EXEC-DEPLOY.yml` exists anywhere the GitHub dispatch can reach; it is not in `.github/workflows` at the pin, so the operator deploy trigger fails today.
